- test(e2e): close the issue #91 P2 tail — in-app notifications, operation logs, system settings, and the synchronous Excel chain. `backend/tests/e2e/` grows from 64 to 69 cases. **test_notification_flow_e2e.py** (REQ-NTF-001): admin system-notice broadcast fans out to active users (created_count ≥ 1), the notice surfaces unread in the inbox, single read flips is_read/read_at, read-all zeroes unread-count, deletion 404s afterwards, blank/PII payloads are rejected (422), and regular users read their inbox but cannot broadcast (403) — the WeCom external send stays blocked, so the REQ remains 开发中. **test_operation_log_and_settings_e2e.py**: the request-logging middleware's audit trail is queryable by actor + module + action (committed rows persist across tests, so assertions filter instead of counting totals), statistics summary honors the days window, malformed date filters 400, unknown log ids 404; system settings round-trip in memory with defaults restored afterwards, unknown keys are silently ignored, type violations 422. **test_excel_export_e2e.py**: the template and both synchronous exports stream real xlsx files (PK magic + content-type + disposition) with zero business data; the async export creates a pending task whose early download is refused (400 任务尚未完成) and unknown tasks 404 on status/download — the full async pipeline needs an independently committed task row, which the transactional e2e harness cannot provide (documented limitation). Validation: backend e2e 69/69 (real PostgreSQL + Redis db15); ruff clean. SSOT: requirements-trace REQ-NTF-001 row + 2026-08-15 note; operation logs / settings / Excel have no dedicated REQ rows (auxiliary endpoints, noted here only).
- test(frontend-e2e): close the issue #92 governance batch — fail-loud isolation preconditions, seed-pinned extraction project, and two new page-flow specs. **Q10**: all 11 conditional `test.skip` sites in `auth/organization-scope-isolation.spec.ts` now fail loud via `rejectPreconditionFailure`/`requirePrecondition` helpers (the latter also restores TypeScript null-narrowing the removed early-returns provided) — a degraded seed environment now errors instead of silently skipping the core isolation tests. **Q12**: removed the dangling `E2E_REQUIRE_AUTH_STATE` env from the ci.yml frontend-e2e job (zero consumers). **Q13**: deleted the empty `asset-manager`/`asset-viewer` role projects from `playwright.config.ts` plus their storage-state writes in global-setup/teardown (no specs ever matched; re-add with real spec directories when role matrices are needed). **Q14**: the CI seed now explicitly creates the extraction project (`ensure_extraction_project`, PRJ-E2E-000001, managed by the seeded party) exposed as `E2E_EXTRACTION_PROJECT_NAME`, and `contract-session.spec.ts` fills that exact name instead of the fuzzy `e2e-project` keyword. Two new unit guards pin the CI contract (`test_ci_workflow.py`: seed-must-provision-extraction-project, dangling-env-must-not-return). **Page blind spots**: new `property-certificate/list.spec.ts` (API-created asset + certificate must render in the list) and `operations/ledger.spec.ts` (API-created project → group → downstream contract chain must surface its GRP- group code on the ledger page). Validation: `pnpm type-check:e2e` clean, oxlint 0 errors on touched files, CI workflow guards 13/13; browser-level execution is delegated to the CI frontend-e2e job (local Playwright lacks the seeded environment, which now intentionally fails loud). **Q15**: new `scripts/check-e2e-retry-rate.mjs` aggregates the Playwright JSON report and fails `pnpm e2e:ci` when the retried-test ratio exceeds the threshold (default 10%, env `E2E_RETRY_THRESHOLD_PERCENT`/`E2E_RETRY_REPORT_PATH`), printing the retried case list — retry-masked flakiness is now a visible gate signal instead of a silent CI retry; verified against a synthetic report (33.3% → fail at 10%, pass at 50%). SSOT: issue #92; CHANGELOG.
- chore(e2e-review): adopt the pre-commit dual-axis code-review findings (issue #91/#92). Standards axis: closed the self-declared admission gap — all 13 suites missing a negative branch gained a CSRF-403 (or analogous anonymous-401) guard test (backend e2e 69→82), `test_party_lifecycle_e2e.py` gained the submitter/approver separation test (regular user cannot approve, admin still can — issue #91 行动项 14 的后半句), `test_role_authorization_e2e.py` gained a regular-user role-creation 403; excel/logs guards now assert `error.code`, not just status; the session-switch trio (`login`/`capture_session`/`restore_session`) extracted into `tests/e2e/session_helpers.py` (was copy-pasted in 3 files); `is_locked_now` decision matrix now has real unit coverage (`TestUserLockSemantics`, 5 cases incl. read-path purity) and its core lines dropped `pragma: no cover`; `require_redis` skips dropped the misleading `allow_module_level=True` (issue #91 Q6); Check 11 gained a maintenance-responsibility comment and testing-standards wording now distinguishes the enforced minimum list from the wider rule text; frontend `resolveSeedPartyId` moved into `helpers/auth.ts` (was duplicated verbatim in both new page specs). Spec axis: corrected the issue #91 record — REQ rows citing backend e2e evidence number **19**, not 21 as the closing comment claimed. Validation: backend e2e 82/82 (real PostgreSQL + Redis db15), unit suites green, type-check:e2e exit 0, docs-lint (incl. Check 11) all passed.
- fix(e2e-ci): drive the frontend-e2e pipeline green through nine CI rounds. The fail-loud conversions (Q10/Q14) exposed real seed and runtime gaps that silent skips had masked for months: (1) baseline format drift broke the lint gates (ruff format 6 backend files incl. the new rbac_service edit, oxfmt 19 frontend files) — repaired and pushed as gate-restoring commits; (2) the frontend-e2e seed now wires asset:read onto the user role (org-scope role-candidate precondition), creates the extraction project (already done in the #92 batch), and explicitly runs init_enum_data (ENVIRONMENT=testing skips the automatic enum bootstrap, so asset creates 422'd with 枚举类型未配置或未激活); (3) org-scope-isolation setup fixes: fallback party creation sent a forbidden status field (PartyCreate extra=forbid), API-created users need admin activation which requires an organization transfer onto a represented party, asset seeding used the wrong field names (property_name/address vs asset_name/address_detail), and three asset-list queries still used the deprecated ownership_id filter; (4) the fail-closed spec now seeds its own party + asset so the organization-default scope (seed org represents LE-000001) cannot make other specs' data visible; (5) contract-session navigates with ?project_id= prefill instead of the flaky ProjectSelect typing and tolerates the query in its URL assertion; (6) ledger/certificate page specs assert real rendered content (contract_id UUID column vs group_code which only appears in the service-fee view; response bodies surfaced on assertion failure). **Real backend defect found and fixed by the e2e run**: AssetResponse serialization touched the unloaded Asset.project relationship on create/review return paths, raising MissingGreenlet under real uvicorn (DATABASE_ERROR, every asset create 500'd); `_with_projection` refreshes project/owner_party/manager_party in place on all seven bare-return sites (get_asset already loaded them). (7) the retry-rate gate first failed on a missing json report: --reporter=dot suppressed the config reporters, then dot,json wrote test-results.json to the cwd — the script now probes both conventions. Validation: full CI pipeline green (lint/type-check/test/build/e2e both sides); unit suites green; asset unit suites 77 passed, asset e2e subset 16 passed locally.

### 2026-08-13

- fix(design): consolidate the design-token system to a single source of truth and bring text contrast to WCAG AA (impeccable audit → harden, branch `fix/design-token-consolidation-20260812`). **Single source of truth**: removed the dead JS theme layer (`src/theme/{light,dark,sharedTokens}.ts`, `src/types/theme.ts`, `ThemeProvider`, `ThemeToggle` + css) whose camelCase CSS vars (`--colorPrimary` etc.) had zero consumers — runtime truth is now only `variables.css` (CSS layer) + `themeConfig.ts` (AntD layer, values aligned). **Dark mode retired**: the four-mechanism half-built dark (CSS media query, `[data-theme]` overrides, the dead JS injection, static AntD algorithm with no switch) is gone; only `prefers-contrast: high` and `prefers-reduced-motion` remain. **rem root drift fixed**: `html { font-size: 14px }` on desktop compressed every rem token 12.5% below documented values — root restored to 16px, body text-size set explicitly (14px desktop / 16px mobile). **Contrast AA**: primary deepened `#1677ff`→`#0e63e5` (white-on-primary 4.10→5.34:1), hover `#4096ff`→`#1169f0` (3.22→4.88:1), tertiary text `#8c8c8c`→`#717171` (3.36→4.88:1); AntD status/preset Tag text overridden via high-specificity `.ant-tag.ant-tag-*:not(.ant-tag-disabled)` to `*-text` deep shades (blue/green/red/gold/cyan/orange/lime/volcano 八色 + success/warning/error/processing 四个状态预设，文字均 ≥4.5:1 on their light tints; 审查修正：antd Tag 无 `info` 预设，原声称的 info 已从声明中移除，`contrastFloors.test.ts` 现逐项钉住 12 个预设的规则存在性与对比度地板). **Gray scale merged**: themeConfig `colorText`/`colorTextSecondary`/`colorTextHeading` converged from the slate family (#334155/#475569/#1e293b) to the neutral family (#262626/#595959) — one gray scale, not two. **Chart palette rebuilt**: `CHART_COLORS` was 10 slots with ~5 distinct colors and three near-identical blues; now 8 cold-spectrum colors with adjacent hue distance ≥30° (blue→cyan→green→gold→red→geekblue→magenta→purple). **tabular-nums** enabled on body/`.ant-table`/`.ant-statistic-content` for ledger number alignment. TDD: new `src/styles/__tests__/contrastFloors.test.ts` (7 tests) parses `variables.css`/`global.css` and asserts the AA floors + preset Tag CSS wiring + chart hue distances + CHART_LABEL_COLORS↔token alignment — written red first, green after tokens. Validation: targeted suite green, full frontend suite 187 files / 2190 tests green, `tsc --noEmit` clean, oxlint clean on changed files. Visual: login page screenshotted at desktop 1440×900 and mobile 390×844; table/tag/chart runtime spot-check still needs the backend seed env (flagged as residual verification gap). SSOT: `DESIGN.md` regenerated (frontmatter primary/tertiary/chart/tag-text tokens, dark-mode section replaced with retirement note, single-source-of-truth + tabular-nums + chart subsections), `.impeccable/design.json` sidecar synced. Dual-axis code review (pre-commit) findings fixed: `verify-token-sync.js` was still reading the deleted `sharedTokens.ts` and would crash the `guard:ui` gate — repointed to assert themeConfig ↔ variables.css overlap tokens (16 keys, rem→px at 16px root), `scan-style-px.config.json` tokenSourceFiles cleaned, page-level `prefers-color-scheme: dark` remnant in `PDFImportPage.module.css` removed, both test providers' `colorPrimary` synced from `#1890ff` to `#0e63e5`, stale `#1677ff` line in DESIGN.md Key Characteristics fixed, `themeConfig.ts` Statistic block formatting repaired, design.json gained the 8 `tag-*-text` colorMeta tokens and its Status Tag demo snippet now uses the AA deep shades, and `contrastFloors.test.ts` extended with a themeConfig↔variables.css same-value assertion block (7 tests). UI guard chain (lint-disable / style-px / token-sync) verified green after fixes. **colorInfo aligned**: themeConfig `colorInfo` `#1677ff`→`#1890ff` matching the pre-existing `variables.css` `--color-info` and DESIGN.md（AntD 层对齐既有 CSS 层取值，无视觉变化，审查补录）。

- fix(analytics-responsive): restore comprehensive distribution charts and the mobile application shell after real desktop/mobile GUI testing. Backend comprehensive analytics now derives four count distributions and four `rentable_area` area distributions from the same party-scoped asset population, groups blank labels as “未分类”, validates all eight arrays through a Pydantic service-boundary contract, rejects incomplete fresh results, and treats incomplete legacy cache entries as observable cache misses; cache identity is versioned and includes normalized perspective plus sorted/deduplicated party IDs. The API no longer retries every deterministic service failure with a duplicate uncached calculation. Frontend analytics requires all eight arrays, uses dedicated required area-distribution types, and fails loudly on contract drift. `AppLayout` selects the existing `MobileLayout` below `48rem`/768px and switches on resize while sharing the same party-scope blocked content; final review restored the mobile global search, real notification center, and shared profile/settings/logout menu instead of activating a static header with dead actions. Replaced 61 invalid CSS-variable `@media` conditions across 41 files with SSOT-equivalent `rem` literals, removed three dead local breakpoint variables, and added a repository-wide media-query guard. The mobile Playwright smoke path fixes its viewport at `390×844` and asserts the desktop sidebar is absent and the navigation drawer opens/closes. Validation: affected backend analytics regressions 112 passed; frontend analytics/page/style regressions 25 passed; layout/header regressions 26 passed; frontend lint, type-check, UI guard, token sync, production build, backend import with process-local Redis disabled, query-param drift, test-credential guard, docs-lint, and `git diff --check` passed. Broad backend run reached 4162 passed with two stale analytics tests subsequently corrected and the affected suite rerun green; broad frontend run reached 2193 passed with one unrelated party-lifecycle timing failure that passed twice in isolation. Formal GUI regression remains incomplete: the in-app browser broker repeatedly returned response-ID mismatches on visible click/screenshot operations; the Playwright smoke reached global setup but local Chromium 1208 is absent (only 1228 is installed), so neither blocker is reported as a visual pass. Final two-axis review remediation moved the active-contract analytics query from service-owned SQL into `contract_crud.list_active_for_analytics`, aligned the shell/CSS boundary to mobile `<768px` with explicit 767/768 regression coverage, synchronized the hardened primary/hover/tertiary token values in the maintained frontend design guide, and added non-empty component tests proving all four count and four `rentable_area` distributions reach their chart adapters; the reported design-system “scope creep” was not adopted because dark-mode retirement, contrast hardening, and the eight-color chart palette were explicit earlier requirements in this same task. Post-review validation: backend analytics/contract CRUD suites 94 passed; frontend analytics/layout suites 99 passed; targeted Ruff, mypy, TypeScript, Oxlint, UI guard, formatting, backend import, query-param drift, test-credential guard, docs-lint, and `git diff --check` passed. SSOT: analytics API/domain/traceability contracts and frontend responsive-design guidance synchronized. 审查整改（本次复核采纳）：`business_category_*` 分布项此前硬编码的伪造 `occupancy_rate: 0.0`/`avg_annual_income: 0.0` 已删除——api-contract 八组分布口径只承载标签/count/percentage 或 total_area/area_percentage/average_area，无数据来源的字段不得透传；连带移除仪表盘「业态类别出租率」图表（此前恒为零值柱）并将导出（Excel/CSV/HTML）的业态列由「出租率(%)」改为「占比(%)」；`ASSET_ANALYTICS_BATCH_SIZE=1000` 分页循环（替代单次 limit=10000 拉取）补录；后端测试新增契约守卫断言伪造字段缺席（TDD red→green），前端 service/export/grid/dashboard 测试同步收敛。

- chore(tooling): sync the workspace ZCode skills from `mattpocock/skills` at upstream commit `84fdeff`. Updated 35 upstream skill directories under `.agents/skills` (including supporting references, scripts, templates, and `agents/openai.yaml` files), added the 10 skills newly present upstream, and preserved the project-local `impeccable` skill. Verified all 35 upstream skill directories match the source tree exactly; no application code changed.

- fix(tooling): expose the workspace skills through the native `.zcode/skills` root used by the ZCode skills page. Added 35 project-level skill entries alongside the existing `impeccable` entry, with no changes to skill content; all 36 `SKILL.md` files resolve from the native root and no broken links remain.

### 2026-08-11

- chore(quality): add blocking frontend Params/FastAPI Query drift gate (issue #84). New standard-library-only `scripts/check_query_param_drift.py` maintains four explicit, auditable contracts: parties, contract groups, ledger entries, and projects. It AST-parses backend positional and keyword-only query-compatible parameters (including `Annotated[..., Query()]` and excluding explicit Header/Body/Cookie/Path/Form/File inputs), rejects mapped FastAPI parameter-marker import aliases, `Query(alias=...)` or keyword expansion and unmarked required parameters until their wire contract can be proven, extracts exactly one non-inherited/non-merged frontend `*Params` interface without counting comments or nested object properties, requires the mapped service method's `params` input to carry that exact interface annotation from the mapping's type file, and verifies the actual top-level `apiClient` request surface rather than an arbitrary local variable. Each contract now also proves its mapped backend route decorator HTTP method/local path and its frontend request HTTP method/final path; FastAPI route proof requires a unique top-level `APIRouter(...)` receiver binding, while frontend paths resolve only literal URLs, top-level constants, statically-resolvable `this` fields, and `API_ENDPOINTS` entries; the ledger contract additionally proves that the public handler declares `Depends(resolve_ledger_query_params)`. Object-style outbound whitelists are enumerated only when every field forwards its matching `params.<field>` input, and conditional spreads are accepted only for proven same-field non-empty guards; input/request-object mutation, point/optional-chain/computed-member `Object` or `Reflect` calls targeting `params`, direct or delayed method-input/request-object aliases (including parenthesized and destructured forms), and unknown helper calls in bare/member/optional/computed, parenthesized, `bind`-chained, or generic-call forms receiving method input or request objects are rejected. Mapped outbound requests are accepted only at the service method's direct statement scope or in its direct `try` block; functions, callbacks, object methods, `catch` blocks, and expression-bodied arrows are non-provable nested scopes. `params: params` is accepted only when it is the unchanged method input, while dynamic forwarding is accepted only when a top-level helper body exactly matches the ledger's provable `Object.entries(params)` normalizer (it may discard only null/empty values and must preserve every remaining key). Ambiguous/non-top-level backend symbols or shadowed frontend bindings, captured/imported dynamic helpers, commented/string request decoys, nested configs, config spreads, mutable whitelists, direct or `Reflect.deleteProperty` request/input mutation, unsupported object spread/computed/shorthand properties, mapped service destructuring/regex-literal source or protected-object template interpolation, FastAPI markers without direct `fastapi`/`fastapi.params` provenance or with assignment/loop/`with as`/exception-handler rebinding, unmarked complex optional parameters, interface inheritance/merging, invalid UTF-8 source, and every unsupported source shape fail loudly rather than relying on incomplete TypeScript scope inference. Backend-only, frontend-only, whitelist-only/missing fields, unprovable forwarding, and parse failures all fail non-zero with both compared source paths and available lines, directly preventing #81's `review_status` type-present-but-not-sent regression. TDD: 71 tests cover clean current contracts, each drift direction, positional/keyword-only/Annotated Query and explicit non-query FastAPI fields, direct/aliased-import/keyword-expansion Query ambiguity, required-param uncertainty and backend symbol ambiguity, request-whitelist omission/non-zero semantics, method-input/request-object mutation (including `Reflect.deleteProperty`, `Reflect.defineProperty`, and `Object.defineProperties`), config spreads, unsupported request forms and conditional spreads, dynamic-field dropping/captured or shadowed helpers, direct input mutation/rebinding, route/request method-path mismatches, dependency disconnects, input aliases, unknown helper calls, FastAPI marker rebindings, unmarked complex optional parameters, direct and nested outbound request scope (including typed, defaulted, destructured, generic, generator, object-method, and expression-arrow forms), direct `try` support, FastAPI router-binding proof, dual-source diagnostics, comments/nested interface fields, interface structure restrictions, and source parse locations, mismatched or non-mapped-source `params` type annotations, unproven local or method-parameter `apiClient` rebinding, FastAPI `with as` marker shadowing, and protected input/container aliasing. Gate is integrated into `make check`, `make ci-gate`, and the blocking CI API Consistency job through `make check-query-param-drift`; AGENTS/scripts usage docs updated. Validation: focused checker suite 71 passed; `make check-query-param-drift`, targeted Ruff/format, `docs-lint`, diff whitespace, frontend CI test suite, and frontend build passed. Full `make check` remains blocked before this gate by pre-existing Ruff import-order errors in `backend/src/api/v1/__init__.py` and `backend/tests/unit/services/document/test_contract_extraction_workflow.py`; frontend type-check passed.

- feat(party): migrate the remaining approved-party selectors to server-side filtering (wayfinder #82, #81 follow-up). Three isomorphic client-side filters are gone: `OrganizationBindingDrawer` and `OrganizationPartyScopeBatchModal` export a shared-pattern `fetchEligibleRepresentedParties` that now sends `review_status: 'approved'` in the `searchParties` request (was: fetch 20 rows + `.filter(review_status === 'approved')`, with the same "first 20 rows fill with non-approved parties" gap); `UserPartyBindingModal` passes `review_status: 'approved'` to `getParties({ limit: 500 })` and drops its `.filter`. No backend change — the server-side param shipped by #81 already serves these. TDD: fetcher unit tests assert the request carries `review_status=approved` and the result is passed through unfiltered (draft row present in the mock), user-management tests flip from "draft party absent from dropdown" (client-filter behavior) to "request carries review_status + draft row passed through" — 4 red first. Validation: organization/user-management suites 14 passed, full frontend suite green, tsc clean, lint at baseline (1 pre-existing warning). SSOT: CHANGELOG only (no contract delta — server contract unchanged).

- feat(party): server-side `review_status` filtering for the approved-party selectors (wayfinder #81, spec #71 decision B contract alignment). `GET /api/v1/parties` now declares and passes through `review_status` (draft|pending|approved|rejected), with the filter branch in `crud/party.py get_parties` (API → service → crud, no endpoint business logic); the PDF import confirmation page's four party selectors (运营方/产权方/出租方/承租方) now query `searchParties(query, { review_status: 'approved', ... })` and the client-side `.filter(party => party.review_status === 'approved')` is removed — closing the "first 20 rows fill with non-approved parties and approved ones become unsearchable" gap, and `PartyListParams.review_status` finally has a real backend receiver. Review caught one blocking gap before merge: `partyService.getParties`'s request whitelist dropped `review_status`, so the real HTTP request never carried the param (client filter already deleted → non-approved parties would leak into the selectors); fixed by forwarding `review_status` in the whitelist, with an apiClient-level assertion added to `partyService.test.ts` (the fetcher test alone mocked the service and could not catch whitelist drops). TDD: backend API test (approved+draft rows, only approved returned), crud SQL-branch compile assertion, layering/service pass-through assertions updated; frontend fetcher test asserts the request carries `review_status=approved` and the result is passed through unfiltered (draft row present in the mock), service test asserts the apiClient params — red first. Validation: backend party suites 78 passed, frontend party/PDFImport suites 21+22 passed, ruff clean, tsc clean, lint at baseline, docs-lint 10 PASS. SSOT: api-contract §主体列表 row documents `review_status`, requirements-trace REQ-DOC-001 evidence + description updated, CHANGELOG. (Gate gap noted in the ticket — frontend-type vs backend-query drift still has no lint coverage; three isomorphic client-side filters in organization/user-binding modals tracked by #82.)

- fix(backend): keep native date types when creating payment flows (G2 dry-run 5.3). The endpoint passed `payload.model_dump(mode="json")` to the service, stringifying `occurred_on` — asyncpg rejected the DATE parameter (`'str' object has no attribute 'toordinal'`) and flow creation 500'd. Changed to `model_dump()` (python mode keeps date/Decimal). TDD: new API test asserting the service receives a `date` object — red (str) before, green after; ledger API + payment-flow service 51/51, ruff clean; HTTP re-verified 200, allocation to 2026-08 (contract_ledger_entry) 200, ledger entry now paid 50000/paid — the full G2 payment-recording chain (create flow → allocate → derived paid status) works. Acceptance checklist G2 row, dry-run report §5.3, issues index updated. Validation: docs-lint passed.
- docs(traceability): verify the G2 scan-upload chain in the acceptance checklist. `POST /api/v1/extraction-sessions` accepted a generated contract-scan PDF (multipart with UTF-8 Chinese form values — plain curl on Windows mangles them, httpx worked), staged it and returned `status=ready_for_review` (session then cancelled via `/cancel` 204, temp file cleaned). Empty candidate fields are the expected local behavior (`DOCUMENT_LLM_ENABLED=false` — DeepSeek disabled per capabilities stages; manual full-entry path applies; candidate extraction + confirm needs DeepSeek configured to re-verify). Remaining G2 items registered: candidate extract→confirm (needs DeepSeek), ledger payment recording (ACC-023 allocation). Validation: docs-lint passed.
- fix(backend): contract creation now returns 201 instead of 500-after-persist (G2 dry-run 5.1). `add_contract_to_group` builds ORM objects with enum *names* (`LESSEE`/`UPSTREAM`/`ACTIVE`), so the same-session `get_contract_detail` (identity-map hit) fed string attributes into `ContractDetail`/`ContractSummary` whose enums are Chinese values — ValidationError → 500 while contracts/rent terms/12 ledger entries were already committed. Added a shared `_coerce_enum_name` + `field_validator(mode="before")` on both response schemas (name→value, enum objects pass through, ORM objects untouched). TDD: new `TestContractDetailEnumCoercion` (2 cases) failed first with the exact ValidationError, passed after; contract service 67/67, API layering 14/14, ruff clean; HTTP re-verified 201 with 承租/上游/生效. Also added `logger.exception(..., exc_info=exc)` to the endpoint catch so original exceptions are now visible in logs (was swallowed by `internal_error(original_error=...)`). Environment note: after the fix HTTP still 500 because three stale uvicorn workers from different restarts were alive simultaneously (dead reloaders left ghost netstat entries; requests routed randomly) — `taskkill //F //T` on the real worker PIDs and a single clean start fixed it.
- docs(issues): register the G2 dry-run findings in the acceptance dry-run report (§5 appendix): (5.1) contract creation API returns 500 while data is fully persisted — `add_contract_to_group` builds the ORM object with enum *names* (`LESSEE`/`UPSTREAM`/`ACTIVE`), the same-session `get_contract_detail` hits the identity-map-cached object with string attributes, and `ContractDetail.model_validate` rejects them against Chinese enum values (`承租`/`上游`/`生效`); reproducible via HTTP (UP-001/UP-002 both 500, contracts + rent terms + 12 ledger entries all committed), root-caused by local full-chain reproduction (`ValidationError: 3 validation errors for ContractDetail`); fix direction: `mode="before"` name→value normalization on `ContractDetail`/`ContractSummary` (TDD), matching `ProjectResponse.coerce_project_model`. Also noted: `internal_error("添加合同失败", original_error=...)` never logs the original exception (observability gap). (5.2) contract-center list "新建合同关系" button navigates to a project-context-less page that refuses submission (UX). Acceptance checklist G2 row updated. Validation: docs-lint passed.
- docs(traceability): record the G2 (contract/agreement capability) dry-run preview in the MVP acceptance checklist. Browser-tested: contract-center list renders (empty state), the new-contract-relation form is fully usable (revenue modes, operator/owner parties, linked assets, settlement rules, revenue attribution — project-context constraint works, requires launching from a project detail with `?project_id=`). Full chain (scan upload → AI parse prefill → manual confirm → ledger generation → payment recording) still requires a real contract plus parser service configuration. Validation: docs-lint passed.
- fix(backend): fill `asset_count` on project list responses. `search_projects` never populated the field (schema default 0), so the project list UI always showed "关联资产 0" and mislabeled every project "待补绑定" (G1 dry-run item 3.3). Added `project_crud.get_asset_counts(project_ids)` — one grouped SQL over active `project_assets` rows, no N+1 — and `search_projects` now bulk-sets it on returned ORM items. Remaining known display gap (product decision): project list "所有方主体" column reads the retired `party_relations` and is always empty.
- docs(issues)/chore(maintenance): close the G1 acceptance dry-run item 3.2 by backfilling project entities from asset `project_name`. New idempotent runner `backend/scripts/maintenance/seed_projects_from_assets.py` creates 11 projects via `ProjectService.create_project` (codes `PRJ-LE000001-202608-0001~0011`, manager party = the environment's only entity 广州国有资产管理集团有限公司) and links 19 active `project_assets` rows (explicit `db.commit()` after the loop — scope-exit implicit commit proved unreliable for the last item). Verified: project API returns 11, asset detail derives `manager_party_id` again (REQ-AST-002 semantics), browser shows the project detail page fully rendering operating summaries (asset count, rentable area, payment summary, vacancy risk derivation, related-asset and lease tables). New finding 3.3 registered: project list `asset_count` is always 0 (backend `search_projects` never fills it) so the UI mislabels projects as "待补绑定"; the "所有方主体" column reads the retired `party_relations` (display semantics to be confirmed by product). Acceptance checklist G1 row and issues index updated. Validation: docs-lint passed.
- fix(frontend): render the asset owner party on the detail page when the backend only returns `ownership_entity`. `AssetDetailInfo.tsx` read `asset.owner_party_name ?? '-'`, but the backend detail schema returns `ownership_entity` (never `owner_party_name`), so 权属方 always showed "-" (found by the G1 acceptance dry-run, `docs/issues/2026-08-09-mvp-g1-acceptance-dryrun.md` §3.1). Added the same fallback chain the list page uses (`owner_party_name ?? ownership_entity ?? '-'`). TDD: new test (backend-only `ownership_entity` renders the owner name) failed first (1 failed / 46 passed), passed after the fix (47/47). Validation: oxlint 0 warnings, frontend type-check passed, browser-verified on the live detail page (权属方 shows 广州国有资产管理集团有限公司).
- docs(traceability): record the first G1 acceptance dry-run in the MVP acceptance checklist (`docs/traceability/mvp-acceptance-checklist.md`). Browser-tested with the `admin` account: asset list/detail rendering verified, lease-status and customer-summary sections render completely (empty-state only, no active contracts); two findings registered in `docs/issues/2026-08-09-mvp-g1-acceptance-dryrun.md` — (1) asset detail "权属方" always shows "-" because `AssetDetailInfo.tsx:98` reads `owner_party_name` while the backend detail schema returns `ownership_entity` (contract mismatch), (2) project entities are missing from seed data (0 projects while 19 assets carry `project_name`), blocking the G1 project-side acceptance. `issues/README.md` index registered. Validation: docs-lint passed.
- docs(issues): move the 2026-08-09 blank-page diagnosis report from `docs/issues/` to `docs/archive/issues/` (resolved reports belong in the archive per the issues-index governance — `docs-lint` Check 8 rejects active reports that are not indexed) and register it in `docs/archive/issues/README.md`. `make docs-lint` 10/10 PASS after the move.
- docs(plans): commit the shelved anydoc office-doc parsing evaluation and the restored active-plans index entry (the files recorded in the 2026-08-08 changelog entry had not been committed). `docs/plans/2026-08-08-anydoc-office-doc-parsing.md` (status ⏸) plus the plans/README.md active-plans table entry are now in the repo.
- docs(issues): archive the 2026-08-09 blank/frozen page diagnosis as `docs/issues/2026-08-09-fe-blank-pages-diagnosis.md`. Records the disproven rAF-freeze hypothesis (strict init-script patch experiment), the confirmed root cause of the blank pages (unregistered routes rendering `null` with no `*` fallback — canonical paths are `/contract-center/list`, `/system/parties`, `/operations/ledger`), the backup `/list[Any]` route defect, and the suspected WebBridge background-tab DOM-snapshot artifact behind the one "frozen page" observation. Includes the point-check guideline: verify the URL is a registered route before treating a blank page as a defect.
- test(frontend): add a main-pages render smoke suite (`tests/e2e/smoke/main-pages-render.spec.ts`). Locks the canonical pages — `/dashboard`, `/assets/list`, `/contract-center/list`, `/operations/ledger`, `/system/parties` — to render their content markers inside `<main>` (not blank, not 404), and locks dead routes (`/no-such-page-xyz`) to render the 404 page instead of a silently blank `<main>`: the exact regression surface the 2026-08-09 daily point-check's "5 blank pages" report maps to (it had probed unregistered paths). Validation: suite 6 passed locally via a temporary local config driving the installed chromium full build (the project-pinned 1208 headless-shell download was still pending); `type-check:e2e` clean.
- fix(frontend): render a 404 page for unmatched protected routes instead of a silently blank `<main>`. React Router had no `*` fallback inside `App.tsx`, so any dead URL (`/contracts`, `/parties`, `/ledger`, `/system`, …) matched nothing and rendered `null` — a white void with no error surface, exactly what the 08-09 daily point-check reported as "5 core pages blank" (the checked paths were not registered routes; the canonical ones are `/contract-center/list`, `/system/parties`, `/operations/ledger`). Added `pages/NotFoundPage.tsx` (antd `Result` 404 + return-to-dashboard button) and the `<Route path="*">` fallback. Validation: new `App.notfound.test.tsx` renders `<App />` with mocked auth/query providers, navigates a dead route, and asserts the 404 page appears — red before the fix, green after; routes suite 10 passed, oxlint 0 warnings, type-check pass; browser-verified live: 5 dead URLs show the 404 page, canonical routes render normally. Diagnosis note: the alternative root-cause hypothesis (AnimatePresence `mode="wait"` exit animation frozen by throttled rAF in background tabs) was tested with a strict rAF-frozen Playwright experiment and disproven — framer-motion renders routes without rAF ticks.
- fix(backend): correct the backup list route registered as the literal `@router.get("/list[Any]")` (typed with a stray FastAPI-param-looking suffix) back to `/list`. `GET /api/v1/system/backup/list` was 404 while the accidental `/system/backup/list[Any]` path was reachable. Validation: new route-table regression test in `test_backup_layering.py` (asserts `/list` registered and `/list[Any]` absent) — red before the fix; backup suites 39 passed, ruff check clean; live backend re-probed after restart: `/list` → 401 (auth gate, route exists), `/list[Any]` → 404.
- fix(e2e): hoist the `E2E-ORG-ROOT` organization code into the shared `E2E_ORG_CODE` env var to close the cross-file magic-string coupling. All three consumers now read one source with the same default: ① the `frontend-e2e` job's `env` block in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` defines `E2E_ORG_CODE: E2E-ORG-ROOT` (the explicit definition point); ② the inline CI seed reads it via `os.getenv("E2E_ORG_CODE", "E2E-ORG-ROOT")` (same pattern as `E2E_ADMIN_USERNAME`); ③ the backend pytest fixture `backend/tests/e2e/conftest.py` reads it for its organization seed, and `access-denied.spec.ts` resolves it from `process.env.E2E_ORG_CODE` with the same default — the spec's not-found error message now reports the actual code in use. A seed code rename now only needs to touch the job env block; local runs and the `backend-e2e` job (which sets no E2E_* vars) keep the previous behavior via the default. Validation: `type-check:e2e` clean, oxlint 0 warnings/errors, oxfmt format-check clean on the spec; ruff check clean on `conftest.py`; CI E2E behavior unchanged (env default matches the old literal).
### 2026-08-08

- fix(ci): run the main-only integration suite without the full-suite coverage gate. The pytest `addopts` carries `--cov-fail-under=70`, which a standalone `tests/integration/` run cannot reach (~46% — the coverage gate belongs to the Backend Tests job that runs the whole suite), so the job kept failing right after all 297 tests passed. The step now runs `pytest tests/integration/ -v --no-cov`. Validation: main CI Pipeline fully green — all 9 jobs success (Backend/Frontend Lint & Type-Check, Backend/Frontend Tests, Backend/Frontend E2E, Integration Tests, API Consistency, CI Summary); Security Scanning success.

- fix(ci): unblock the main-only Integration Tests gate, which had been skipping every test and then failing the coverage floor. The `integration` job exported only `DATABASE_URL`, but `tests/integration/conftest.py` resolves its database from `INTEGRATION_TEST_DATABASE_URL`/`TEST_DATABASE_URL` (fallback `backend/.env`, absent in CI) — so all 230 tests skipped and `--cov-fail-under=70` failed the run at ~0% coverage. The job now exports `TEST_DATABASE_URL` alongside `DATABASE_URL`. Validation: locally reproduced the broken env (all-skip + coverage fail) and confirmed the suite itself is healthy (297 passed with the env var set); the gate will be re-verified by the next main push.

- fix(ci): complete the Frontend E2E alignment with the party-scope/organization cutover so the CI Pipeline's last red step (Frontend E2E Tests) is green. The inline E2E seed in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` had drifted from the 08-04/08-06 model changes in five places, and the access-denied spec provisioned its regular user against the retired lifecycle. Fixes: ① seed party code `E2E-LEGAL-ADMIN` → `LE-000001` (must match the `ck_parties_code_format` constraint `^LE-[0-9]{6}$`); ② seed `User` kwargs `default_organization_id` → `organization_id` (column renamed by the cutover); ③ seed `UserPartyBinding` dropped the removed `is_primary` kwarg; ④ seed now links `E2E-ORG-ROOT` to the seeded party (`represented_party_id` + `represented_party_perspective="owner"`, idempotent) and creates/updates the party with `review_status="approved"` — both required for the organization branch of `PartyScopeResolver` to yield an effective scope; ⑤ `access-denied.spec.ts` provisions its regular user through the new lifecycle (create → `POST /auth/users/{id}/organization/preview` → `PUT /auth/users/{id}/organization` commit → `POST /auth/users/{id}/activate`) instead of expecting API-created users to be immediately active. Validation: CI Pipeline fully green on develop — Backend Lint (mypy 0 errors / bandit 0 / ruff) , Backend Tests, Backend E2E, Frontend Lint & Type-Check, Frontend Tests, Frontend E2E, API Consistency; `type-check:e2e` + oxlint + oxfmt clean locally for the spec change.

- fix(ci): align the Frontend E2E seed script's party code with the `ck_parties_code_format` check constraint (added by the 08-04 party-scope cutover migration, which the inline CI seed in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` never caught up with — legal_entity codes must match `^LE-[0-9]{6}$`). `PARTY_CODE` changed from `E2E-LEGAL-ADMIN` to `LE-000001`; no e2e spec references the old code. Validation: CI Pipeline Backend Lint (mypy/bandit/ruff), Backend Tests, Backend E2E, Frontend Lint/Type-Check/Tests all green — this was the last red step after clearing the 68-error mypy backlog and the bandit B105 false positives.

- fix(backend): clear the 68 pre-existing mypy errors that had been red-lighting the CI `Run MyPy type checking` step (latent since before this branch — local `make check` does not run mypy, so the drift accumulated unnoticed across the 08-06/07 party-scope and organization-move features). Fixes, all semantics-neutral: ① five commit-receipt CRUDs (`user_party_scope_commit` / `party_lifecycle_commit` / `organization_move_commit` / `user_organization_transfer_commit` / `user_party_scope_batch_commit`) switched from explicit-kwargs model construction to the codebase's established `Model(**{...})` dict-unpack idiom — the SQLAlchemy mypy plugin generates no `__init__` for these models (root cause of the 51 `call-arg` errors; `crud/asset.py` already works around the same plugin gap with `Asset(**dict)`); ② removed the dead `owner_party_id=None` argument the 08-08 cleanup missed in `services/search/service.py` (field was dropped from `ProjectSearchRequest`, Pydantic's default `extra=ignore` masked it at runtime); ③ `crud/auth.py` + `user_management_service.py` assign `AccountType.HUMAN` instead of the bare string; ④ `lifecycle_change_service._normalize_operation` returns `PartyLifecycleOperation` via cast (it already validated the set); ⑤ `party_scope_resolver` casts the three `RowMapping`-returning query results back to `Mapping[str, Any]` (RowMapping is runtime-compatible but not assignable in mypy) and casts `perspective` to `ScopeMode`; ⑥ `organization/service.get_representing_organizations` casts the NOT-NULL old-style column attrs (conservatively Optional in static types) into the required schema fields; ⑦ `PartyIdentifierService` annotates `_encryptor` with the `_DeterministicEncryptor` protocol type; ⑧ the preflight script narrows `parent_id` reads with isinstance. Validation: `mypy src/` 0 errors across 387 files, ruff check + `ruff format --check src/` clean, full backend suite 4370 passed / 26 skipped / 0 failed (identical to baseline).

- style(backend): ruff-format the `tests/` directory to clear the last format drift debt (150 files reformatted, mechanical only — the drift was outside CI's gate scope, which checks `src/` only). Validation: `ruff format --check tests/` clean, `ruff check .` clean, full backend suite 4370 passed / 26 skipped / 0 failed (identical to the pre-format baseline).

- fix(frontend): address code-review findings on the exceljs migration. `parsePartyImportWorkbook` now maps date cells to Excel serial numbers and hyperlink/rich-text cells to their display text (faithful `sheet_to_json` raw-value semantics — previously dates came through as JS `Date` objects and hyperlink cells as `[object Object]`), with a new regression test locking the serial-number contract; `exportToExcel` restores the shared column-width constants (5 inline copies) and revokes the blob object URL after download; the ana001 test restores its `URL.createObjectURL` spy in both the capture helper and the CSV case. Validation: partyImport + ana001 suites 6 passed, oxlint 0 warnings, type-check pass, `format:check` clean.

- refactor(frontend): replace xlsx 0.18.5 with exceljs 4.4.0 to clear the last 2 high xlsx Dependabot alerts (the npm package is abandoned upstream — no patched release exists; SheetJS now ships only via its own CDN). `parsePartyImportWorkbook` now loads the first worksheet via `workbook.xlsx.load` + header-row mapping (same semantics as `sheet_to_json`: first row = headers, blank rows skipped, missing cells as empty strings); `exportToExcel` builds sheets with `addWorksheet`/`addRows` + per-sheet `columns` widths and downloads via `writeBuffer` → Blob → object URL (replacing `XLSX.writeFile`). Tests reworked from the xlsx-mock shape to real exceljs round-trips: `partyImport.test.ts` builds its fixture with exceljs, and `analyticsExportService.ana001.test.ts` captures the exported bytes, reloads them with exceljs, and asserts the ANA-001 fields inside the actual `概览统计` worksheet. `pnpm.overrides` additionally pins `uuid` ^11.1.1 (exceljs depends on uuid ^8.3.0, all <11.1.1 vulnerable). Result: `pnpm audit` reports zero known vulnerabilities (down from 122 across both ecosystems at branch start). Validation: full vitest suite pass, production build pass, `pnpm check` full static chain pass (lint/guards/type-checks/authz checks/format).

- refactor(frontend): migrate react-router-dom from 6.30.4 to 7.18.2 to clear the last 3 moderate react-router Dependabot alerts (6.x has no patched release — all three GHSA require ≥7.18). The app uses the declarative `<BrowserRouter>/<Routes>` pattern (no data router), so the migration is a package bump plus removal of the now-obsolete `future={{ v7_startTransition, v7_relativeSplatPath }}` prop on `BrowserRouter` (both behaviors are v7 defaults). Validation: type-check, oxlint, `pnpm check` full static chain, and route-focused component tests (AuthGuard/AppLayout/AppSidebar/ErrorBoundary/routes) pass.

- chore(deps): upgrade frontend dependencies to clear 59 npm Dependabot alerts (`pnpm audit`: 31 → 5 remaining, all without upstream fixes within the current majors). Direct bumps within existing semver ranges: axios 1.13.6→1.19.0, lodash 4.17.23→4.18.1, vite 6.4.1→6.4.3, react-router-dom 6.30.3→6.30.4. Dev-tooling transitive chains (stylelint→table→ajv→fast-uri, stylelint→postcss/nanoid, stylelint→cosmiconfig→js-yaml, stylelint→file-entry-cache→flatted, type-coverage→minimatch→brace-expansion, rollup-plugin-visualizer→picomatch, jsdom→ws, @babel/*) pinned patched versions via `pnpm.overrides` (postcss ^8.5.26, fast-uri ^3.1.5, js-yaml ^4.3.1, flatted ^3.4.2, nanoid ^3.3.17, brace-expansion ^5.0.9, picomatch ^4.0.4, ws ^8.21.0, @babel/core ^7.29.6, @babel/plugin-transform-modules-systemjs ^7.29.4) — plain `pnpm up --latest` cannot move these because the declaring dev tools pin exact vulnerable versions (e.g. stylelint's lock entry records postcss 8.5.6). Remaining (deferred, see below): react-router 6.x 3×moderate (no patched 6.x exists — requires the v7 migration) and xlsx 2×high (npm releases abandoned upstream — requires a replacement library). Validation: oxlint 0 warnings/errors on 585 files, type-check pass, full vitest suite pass, production build pass.

Pure lock refresh within the existing `>=` minimum bounds — no `pyproject.toml` constraint changes: starlette 0.49.3→1.5.0 (via fastapi), cryptography 46.0.5→50.0.0, pillow 12.1.1→12.3.0, pyjwt 2.10.1→2.13.0, python-multipart 0.0.22→0.0.32, nltk 3.9.3→3.10.2 (pulled by safety 3.7.0→3.8.1), urllib3 2.6.3→2.7.0, requests 2.32.5→2.34.2, pytest 8.4.2→9.1.1, click 8.3.0→8.4.2, plus idna/mako/python-dotenv/pygments and new truststore. Also repaired the local venv, which had been corrupted by an interrupted `uv sync --extra dev` (the project sync contract is `--extra dev --extra document-processing`; the wrong-extra sync stripped pymupdf/rapidocr/onnxruntime and left `fitz` as a broken empty namespace — `uv sync --frozen --extra dev --extra document-processing --reinstall-package pymupdf` restored it). - fix(frontend): align the asset-history service return type with the flattened runtime response and clear the stale tests left by the owner-party/project-count cleanup. The first real frontend type-check run (previously deferred to CI) exposed that `assetHistoryService.getAssetHistory` / `assetService.getAssetHistory` declared the nested `PaginatedApiResponse<AssetHistory>` while `smartExtract` + `normalizePaginatedData` actually return the flat `{items,total,page,page_size,pages}` structure — `AssetHistory.tsx` papered over the lie with an invalid `as Record<string, unknown>` cast. Added generic `NormalizedPaginatedResponse<T>` (runtime contract of `normalizePaginatedData`), switched both service methods to it, removed the cast and the dead `pagination.total` fallback from the component, and corrected the component test mock from the nested to the flat shape (the old mock never matched production). The first full frontend vitest run (also previously deferred) then exposed 5 stale assertions the cleanup missed: `ProjectList.test.tsx` still expected the removed `project-owner-party-options` query, the removed owner-party filter select, and `searchParties` calls (4 tests + 3 dead mock branches), and `OwnershipDetail.test.tsx` still asserted the removed `project_count` tag — all deleted or aligned; the dead `Party` import in `ProjectList.tsx` was dropped with them. - chore(frontend): pin the package manager to pnpm 10 and declare the approved build scripts. CI pins pnpm 10 (`pnpm/action-setup version: 10`), but local corepack fell back to pnpm 11.9.0 (no `packageManager` field), where `onlyBuiltDependencies` was removed in favor of the `allowBuilds` map in `pnpm-workspace.yaml` and ignored build scripts became a hard error (`ERR_PNPM_IGNORED_BUILDS`, strictDepBuilds default true) — blocking `pnpm install`/`pnpm run` locally and auto-injecting an `allowBuilds` placeholder scaffold into the tracked `pnpm-workspace.yaml`. Added `"packageManager": "pnpm@10.34.5"` so corepack resolves the same major as CI, and kept `pnpm.onlyBuiltDependencies: ["esbuild", "msw"]` (pnpm 10 semantics) so the two postinstall scripts are approved instead of ignored. Validation: `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` exits 0 with no scaffold injection; `pnpm type-check` / `type-check:e2e` / `lint` all pass through the real pnpm entry points; AssetHistory/ProjectList/OwnershipDetail suites 64 passed on the pnpm-10-linked tree.

### 2026-08-07

- chore(deps): bump authlib to 1.7.2 and the vitest family to 4.1.10 to clear both critical Dependabot alerts (GHSA-wvwj-cvrp-7pv5, GHSA-5xrq-8626-4rwp). authlib is a transitive dev-only dependency of `safety` and is not imported at runtime (JWT handling uses PyJWT), so its upgrade is a supply-chain hardening; vitest/@vitest/ui/@vitest/coverage-v8 are frontend test-tooling dev dependencies upgraded to the patched 4.1.x line. Validation: backend history CRUD / asset service / history API / layering suites 80 passed on the existing venv (authlib is not runtime-linked); `uv lock` resolves cleanly; frontend lockfile resolves 817 packages with vitest 4.1.10. Frontend runtime verification (vitest run on 4.1.10) is deferred to CI: local `pnpm install` is blocked by the WorkBuddy sandbox (safe-delete shim + Windows symlink restriction), which is an environment limitation, not a dependency issue.

- fix(asset): stabilize the change-history pagination ordering with a deterministic tie-breaker. `HistoryCRUD.get_multi_with_count_async` now orders by `operation_time DESC, id DESC` so the LIMIT/OFFSET pagination (count and list are separate queries) cannot duplicate or skip rows when `operation_time` ties; CRUD tests now assert the `id` secondary sort, the bound-value `operation_type = 'create'` filter on both count and list queries, and the absence of the filter clause when `change_type` is None. Validation: history CRUD / asset service / history API / layering suites 80 passed; ruff clean.

- feat(party): add all-or-nothing user Party-scope batch preview/commit. Batch proposals require 2-100 unique users, run per-user `manage_party_scope` authorization, lock users and bindings, revalidate every affected state, then write all bindings and one idempotent receipt in a single transaction. Preview storage preserves the original close/update proposal shape, and idempotent retries restore targets from the durable receipt. Also add the documented self/administrator effective Party-scope diagnostic views: `GET /auth/me/party-scope` omits `node_ref` for self-service, while the administrator view returns structured issues with authorized node references. - feat(organization-party-scope): add an all-or-nothing Organization represented-Party batch preview/commit workflow. Batch proposals require 2-100 unique independent Organizations, run per-Organization `manage_party_scope` authorization, reject ancestor/descendant overlap, lock and revalidate every affected chain and subtree, then write all direct links, histories, and one idempotent receipt in a single transaction. Idempotent retries retrieve their targets from the durable receipt before reauthorizing, so consuming the original preview token does not turn a successful retry into a stale-preview failure. The Organization list now supports multi-select, common Party/perspective proposals, impact preview, required reason, and confirmed commit. - feat(party): complete the approved-Party lifecycle preview/commit slice. Party creation, import, ordinary updates, and quick creation no longer accept status writes. The Party detail page now shows the impact preview and requires a reason-confirmed commit. ### 2026-08-03

- feat(migration): add a read-only, privacy-safe Organization/Party scope cutover preflight and Make target. The gate fails closed on missing source tables, unresolved legacy `organization` Parties with dynamic foreign-key reference counts, PartyHierarchy rows, `headquarters` bindings, organization-less unclassified users, and Party codes outside the target `LE/NP` formats. Focused TDD passes; the current local PostgreSQL snapshot is intentionally blocked by one unclassified active user and one legacy Party code, with no automatic classification or repair applied.
- docs(architecture): complete the ADR/SSOT closeout for ADR-0022 and the active one-cutover implementation plan, mark ADR-0001's internal-organization conclusions as superseded, and align the PRD, domain model, API contract, requirement trace, architecture index, documentation index, and plan index around the Organization/Party boundary and single Party-scope resolver. The five affected requirements are explicitly `开发中` until migration, backend, frontend, and browser evidence exists; the active plan now advances to the data-preflight and model-migration stage.
- docs(domain): clarify that Party is the business-identity axis for ownership, operation, contracting, and tenancy, while Organization is the internal people-and-department hierarchy. Internal departments do not become Party records merely for organization membership or data-scope configuration; each organization may represent at most one legal-entity Party, one legal-entity Party may be represented by multiple organizations, and natural-person Party access requires explicit user binding. Unlinked children inherit the nearest ancestor's represented Party with fail-closed behavior when no ancestor is linked. Active explicit user-Party bindings fully replace this inherited default instead of being silently unioned with it, and every organization-derived default carries an explicit owner or manager perspective rather than an ambiguous any scope.
- docs(domain): restrict Party identity types to `legal_entity` and `individual`. Retire the ambiguous `organization` type without adding `other_organization`; internal departments, project teams, and non-legal-person organizations are outside the Party registry.
- docs(domain): require organization-represented Parties to be approved, active legal entities. A later inactive or deleted Party keeps its auditable link but grants no default scope, raises a configuration error, and blocks ancestor fallback instead of silently switching the effective Party.
- docs(domain): make Organization-to-Party authorization links explicit. Names, ordinary codes, and external references may produce search candidates but cannot activate a link; only an administrator-selected Party ID or a human-confirmed authoritative mapping may establish the relationship, including in bulk operations.
- docs(domain): classify represented-Party changes, default perspective changes, scope-affecting organization moves, human-user transfers, and Party lifecycle actions as sensitive authorization changes. Require a short-lived preview token bound to the actor, target, proposal, and relevant versions; commits add a reason and idempotency key, reject stale previews with 409, audit before/after values, invalidate caches immediately, and keep MVP free of an approval workflow or forced re-login. Bulk range writes are transactional all-or-nothing.
- docs(domain): add immutable `account_type=human|service|system` so organization-membership exceptions are explicit rather than guessed from usernames or roles. Limit each active human user to exactly one internal Organization; service/system accounts must remain organization-less and inherit no organization default scope.
- docs(domain): remove `headquarters` from explicit user-Party bindings so `owner` and `manager` remain the only perspectives. Group-wide access must enumerate Parties explicitly, including through confirmed bulk operations, and cannot expand automatically when the Party hierarchy changes.
- docs(domain): remove `is_primary` from user-Party authorization bindings. Every currently effective explicit binding contributes equally to the complete scope; any future default-display preference must remain separate from authorization resolution.
- docs(domain): store an Organization's direct represented Party and default perspective as the inseparable nullable pair `represented_party_id` and `represented_party_perspective`. Derive inherited effective scope at read time, reuse Organization history for before/after auditing, and avoid a separate relationship table or persisted inherited values.
- docs(domain): make Organization management the only write entry for represented-Party links, show inherited effective Party/perspective/source there, expose only a read-only reverse organization list in Party Center, and rename the existing per-user binding action to "用户数据范围".
- docs(domain): require active users and Organizations to remain on a fully active Organization ancestor chain. Block deactivation until active children and users are handled, avoid automatic cascades, and fail closed with a configuration error when legacy data contains an inactive node in the inheritance path.
- docs(domain): allow user-Party bindings to target only approved active Parties. A time-effective binding whose target later becomes invalid grants nothing and raises a configuration error without triggering Organization fallback; remaining valid explicit bindings still apply, while fallback is reserved for users with no currently effective binding records.
- docs(authz): introduce dedicated `organization:manage_party_scope` and `user:manage_party_scope` actions for represented-Party and explicit-binding changes. Grant them only to `admin`, `system_admin`, and `perm_admin` by default, while preserving the permission administrator's no-business-data and no-business-role-grant boundaries.
- docs(domain): rename the human user's single Organization membership from `default_organization_id` to `organization_id` across the target model. Preserve existing IDs through a one-time column rename, require null for explicit service/system account types, and provide no alias, dual write, or compatibility read.
- docs(migration): forbid automatic conversion of legacy `party_type=organization` rows. Preflight all affected Parties and references, clear disposable data, require explicit reclassification for retained rows, and abort while any unresolved record remains.
- docs(domain): remove the untyped `PartyHierarchy` model, table, APIs, and services from MVP together with `headquarters` scope expansion. Keep internal hierarchy in Organization and defer legal-entity group relationships until relation type and validity semantics are explicitly modeled.
- docs(domain): add dedicated deactivate/reactivate lifecycle actions for approved Parties and forbid their hard deletion. Require impact previews, reasons, and audits; preserve historical references while inactive, block new references and authorization, and restore retained links only through an explicit reactivation confirmation.
- docs(authz): classify all user-Party binding creates, edits, closures, and bulk changes as sensitive authorization changes. Require effective-scope previews and reasons, audit before/after values, invalidate caches immediately, and make scheduled start/end boundaries take effect without re-login or an approval workflow.
- docs(domain): make `Party.code` a concurrency-safe, immutable system identifier using `LE-000001` and `NP-000001` formats. Keep it separate from official identifiers, external references, and Organization codes while retaining it as the stable source for downstream business-code segments.
- docs(domain): promote Party identity data from metadata to first-class `identifier_type` and `identifier_value` fields. Freeze legal types to unified-credit, legal-registration, and foreign-registration identifiers with normalized-value uniqueness; freeze individual types to national ID and passport with encrypted values, masked output, and typed fingerprint uniqueness.
- docs(authz): designate a single `PartyScopeResolver` for unrestricted, explicit-binding, Organization-inherited, and fail-closed scope resolution. Only active built-in `admin`/`system_admin` assignments are range-unrestricted; `perm_admin` remains business-data restricted. Require all query, ABAC, resource-context, notification, and analytics consumers to use the source-aware result; remove guessed mappings, `legacy_org_ids`, and caches that outlive scheduled authorization boundaries. Public analysis uses only `view_mode`; dual-perspective omission resolves to internal `scope_mode=all` rather than guessing a primary binding.
- docs(authz): make missing or invalid Party scope fail business-data APIs with stable 403 reason codes instead of successful empty results. Require a blocking frontend configuration state and privacy-safe diagnostic logs while exempting login, system configuration, and unrestricted administrator access.
- docs(authz): add a read-only effective Party-scope view backed directly by `PartyScopeResolver`. Let users inspect their own source, Organization inheritance, perspectives, Parties, next transition, and structured issues; let system-management roles inspect any user while limiting node references by management permission and always hiding official identifiers, fingerprints, and external references.

### 2026-07-10

Added `GET /api/v1/ledger/service-fees` with contract-group data-scope checks and service-layer tests, exposed service-fee source rent ledgers in `/operations/ledger`, and lets users register service-fee receipts from listed rows. Contract-group scope modes now remain enforced when no effective parties are visible, and manual service-fee receipts no longer refresh an empty group query. Comprehensive analytics now returns `operational_metric_groups` plus `period_attribution_basis/label`, analytics export marks the period attribution basis, due/overdue notification scans are constrained to `terminal_collection`, and contract-expiry notifications use user-facing “合同/协议” wording. Validation: backend targeted pytest `99 passed`; frontend targeted vitest `63 passed`; `corepack pnpm check` passed.
- feat(operations-ledger): start Phase 4 frontend rollout by replacing the global finance-ledger entry with `/operations/ledger`, adding four operations-ledger views, payment-flow allocation actions, terminal follow-up maintenance, service-fee generation/receipt entry points, project-summary jumps, and contract/asset/party links. Added a terminal follow-up API guarded by the ledger update resource.
- feat(operations-ledger): expose Phase 3 backend ledger APIs for operational payment-flow creation, allocation save, and monthly service-fee generation. Extended `GET /api/v1/ledger/entries` and export to filter by contract rent-ledger view, project, active payment-flow occurrence date, and derived payment status, added `ledger_views` / `flow_occurred_on_dates` to query/export evidence, and registered the new ledger authz resource in RBAC seed/backfill coverage.
- test(project): stabilize project-code generation unit coverage by routing month selection through the existing project-service clock helper and freezing the generated-code tests to June 2026.

### 2026-07-08
- feat(operations-ledger): derive `service_fee_source_mismatch` risks for generated service-fee ledgers whose frozen source rent ledgers, calculation base, ratio, amount, or attribution no longer match current allocation-derived rent receipts, or whose frozen source rent ledger is stale after contract correction. Project risks no longer treat unpaid service fees as overdue.
- feat(operations-ledger): preserve manually allocated ledger facts during contract-correction recalculation. CRUD now marks active allocation counts on ledger read models, recalculation and correction reversal skip entries with active allocations, and project risk derivation keeps `ledger_stale_after_correction` visible for allocated entries that no longer match current rent terms.
- feat(operations-ledger): generate service-fee ledgers from direct-lease receipt periods using the entrusted-agreement ratio that fully covers each rent ledger month. The generator now supports historical entrusted agreements, freezes the agreement ID/ratio/calculation base/source period on creation, and fails loudly when a rent ledger month crosses a service-fee ratio interval instead of splitting by day.
- feat(operations-ledger): tighten payment-flow registration and allocation validation. `PaymentFlowService` now rejects unsupported flow types, supports terminal rent receipts, service-fee receipts, and upstream cost payments through the same service seam, validates allocation target view, ledger period, frozen project/owner/operator/currency scope, and amount total, and recalculates stale targets removed from a flow's allocations. Added focused unit coverage for valid and invalid allocation paths.

Updated field-drift mapping and REQ-RNT-006 traceability; added focused model, migration, ledger service, payment flow service, and service fee service tests.

- docs(design): UI/UX 对齐方案 U1+U2 落地 + 两拍板。按 yellowUp「按你的建议做」定：**(a)** 角色工作台做、但仅 4 个 MVP 画像（运营操作员 / 运营管理 / 产权方对接人 / 复核人），招商 / 物业整体 vNext；**(b)** 产权方对接人纳入 MVP（系统数据隔离最强的真实用户，缺则成 §8 隐患）。**U1**（治理）：`docs/design/README.md` 增两条——设计稿须标「对齐 MVP 范围基线」、超 §4 范围标 `vNext`、口径单源只引 PRD/domain-model（防 G1/G5 漂移）；role-dashboard 过时横幅已加。**U2**（任务流）：新增 `docs/design/flows/`——3 条核心任务流贴现有口径成文：`flow-contract-intake`（补录链路：扫描件→解析逐字段确认〔候选不自动绑定 / 会话即用即弃 / 补录即生效〕→台账）、`flow-ledger-settlement`（实收只录金额、状态 / 逾期派生、不翻牌、不红字冲销）、`flow-risk-remediation`（**核心：无「标记已处理」按钮**，风险只读 + 下钻 + 改源、派生自然消除，体现「派生优于手工标记位」）。这 3 条 + S1 画像即 PRD P6/B 缺的最小用户层。U3~U5（任务化工作台前端）待排期。新增 `docs/design/flows/README.md` + 3 个 flow 文档；对齐方案加「决策与进展」节。
- docs(design): 立 2026-06-20 UI/UX 全面分析与对齐方案。挖透现状（前端 ~30 页 = 模块化 IA + `docs/design/` 设计稿）后诊断 G1~G5：**G1 设计 ⟂ MVP 范围错位**——`role-based-dashboard-redesign.md`(6/03) 早于多轮减法，其「招商」（意向/漏斗）「物业运维」（工单/巡检）两工作台及 5 个新 API 超 MVP 范围、引用已删的 `approval/pending`（ADR-0002）；**G2 画像借自通用物管、非系统真实用户轴**——真实轴是（运营方/产权方绑定）×（RBAC），设计完全漏了「产权方对接人」这个系统真实服务且数据隔离最强的画像；**G3** IA 仍模块形、任务视角只停首页提案未落地；**G4** 缺核心任务流（补录链路/实收登记/风险处置）；**G5** 设计资产无 scope 治理随减法漂移。解决方案 S0~S5 + 分批 U1~U5：U1 收口 role-dashboard（标过时 + 招商/物业降 vNext + 修 stale 引用）+ 设计治理（纯文档可立即）、U2 重定义 4 个 MVP 画像 + 3 条核心任务流文档、U3~U5 任务化工作台（运营/管理/产权方/复核，复用现有 API + 仅 1 个 `dashboard/summary` 新 API）。**核心结论：不补 UI/UX，而是对齐**——砍为不存在功能设计的招商/物业、补漏掉的产权方工作台、IA 模块形渐进任务形。已同步给 `role-based-dashboard-redesign.md` 加过时横幅、design README 登记。承接 PRD 质量分析 P6/B（用户层）——S1 画像 + S4 任务流即最小可用用户层。新增 `docs/design/2026-06-20-uiux-analysis-and-alignment.md`。

- docs(prd,issues): 复核 2026-06-20 PRD 质量分析 issue + 再审 PRD——修一处批 3 引入的真 bug + 校正 issue 数字。**PRD bug**：§9「建议验收覆盖」批 3 删 bullet-289（失败重试项）时把相邻两条验收断言黏成一行（「…照常返回。- 搜索结果不返回未授权对象。」缺换行），致「搜索结果不返回未授权对象」被吞进客户双指标条目（docs-lint 不查 bullet 完整性故漏网）；已拆回两条，ripgrep 确认 line 274 独立 bullet、无黏连残留、PRD 共 41 条 bullet。**issue 校正**：NTF-001 实测 1262 字（原写 ~1100、低估）、批 1 削后的 AST-005 实测 701 字（原多处写 ~400、低估）——补「自然地板」洞见：AST-005/RNT-005(642)/RNT-006(623) 削后均 ~650-700 字，复杂 REQ 有篇幅地板，NTF-001 现实收口目标降到 ~650 而非 200；ADR 引用 20 行中「见 ADR」形式 18 处。**坐实**：domain-model 确无 Notification 对象（`通知` 唯一命中在 §4.14 台账段顺带提及）、api-contract §4.12 通知行逐条复制 NTF-001 机制（档位幂等键/`touser`/`related_entity_id`）。其余编辑点（§6.3、§4.10 prose、§9 16-bullet 折叠）复核无黏连。docs-lint 7 项全绿。
- docs(issues): 立 2026-06-20 PRD 文档质量深度分析 issue（📋 待评审）。批 1~5 详略再平衡（解析/附件机制三文档单一化 + Check 7 守卫）收口后，对整篇 `prd.md` 全面复盘，列 P1~P7 排序问题：**P1 REQ-NTF-001 是下一面墙**——§7.10 单格 1262 字（实测、全 PRD 最大）塞档位幂等键 / `days_overdue` 派生 / `related_entity_id` / 企业微信 `touser` 等机制，与开工前 REQ-AST-005 完全同构，`domain-model` 无 Notification 对象（`通知` 仅命中 1 处）、api-contract §4.12 又抄一遍（PRD↔api-contract 跨文档复制），建议建 Notification 对象 + 三文档单一化（决策已定 ADR-0015/0016 + 2026-06-18 系统通知，低风险，可立即）；P2 §7 altitude 方差（CUS-001 一行 vs NTF-001 千字）；P3 表格当散文；P4 Out-of-Scope/§5 防御性沉积；P5 ADR 引用噪音（20 行带 `见 ADR-00xx`）；**P6 根问题——§1 声称业务/设计读者，但 register 满是 `owner_party_id`/`scope_mode`/`related_entity_id`，PRD 已成工程规格穿 PRD 外衣，搬运解决不了，需拍板：收窄 §1 读者声明（A）或补业务面层（B）**；P7 §3.2 成功观测单薄（仅口径一致性一条主观指标、零使用信号）。建议路线批 A=P1（可立即）、批 B=P2+P3、批 C=P4+P5，P6/P7 待 yellowUp 决策。新增 `docs/issues/2026-06-20-prd-document-quality-analysis.md`，登记进 issues README 索引。

### 2026-06-19
- docs(scripts,traceability): PRD 详略再平衡第五批——E 守卫锁死 PRD 不回灌 + 顺修并行联审清理遗留的 trace 死链。`scripts/check_requirements_authority.py` 新增 Check 7「PRD mechanics-token guard」：仅 `docs/prd.md`（不约束 specs——机制本就该落 specs）出现 `\d+MB`/`JPG·JPEG`/`ocr_prefill_confirmed·corrected`/`manual_after_ocr_miss`/`field_sources` 等字段级机制 token 即 FAIL，提示移到 domain-model；token 集选无歧义机制项、近零误报（当前 PRD 全无、加了即过），咬合测试 5 个 pattern 全中。这把前四批「PRD 产品级、机制落 domain-model」成果固化为闸门，防 grill 再深 bloat 回灌。另：运行发现 Check 5（traceability 路径存在性）失败——`requirements-trace.md:48` REQ-RNT-004 测试证据列指向已被并行 ADR-0013 联审清理删除的 `backend/tests/unit/services/contract/test_contract_joint_review.py`（batch-1 时文件尚在、Check 5 仍绿，期间被并行工作删除致死链，非本轮改动引起）；只移除该失效路径（不改 ADR-0013 状态/叙述，留给该工作 owner）以恢复绿。验证：`check_requirements_authority.py` 7 项全绿、`check_field_drift` EXIT=0。**至此 PRD 详略再平衡主体完成（批 1~5）**：解析/附件机制三文档单一化 + 守卫固化；D（7.3/7.4 加厚）评估后不做——「详略不当」已由减法解决，7.3/7.4 概要但完整、其口径正确落在 §6.1/§6/domain-model §6，再加厚反而重复、违背刚收口的单一真相原则。
- docs(api-contract): PRD 详略再平衡第四批——api-contract §4.3/§4.10 跨文档复制收口，机制墙单一化闭环。§4.3「资产产权证」「产权证扫描件解析」与 §4.10「扫描件解析辅助补录」三个巨格此前把 domain-model §2/§4.20~4.23 的机制（20MB/文件类型/下载日志/预览/去重/`field_sources` 枚举/低置信处理/候选不自动绑定/会话不留档）整段复制。削成端点契约 + 引用 domain-model，保留端点专属（证号重复响应契约、`validate_extracted_fields` 闸门、`accept_all`/`should_create_new_asset` 载荷约束、`target_type`、状态码）；§4.10 四个 endpoint 行各瘦身、两段巨型 prose（旧 186/188）折成 1 段引用。grep 确认 `20MB`/文件扩展名/`manual_after_ocr_miss` 现仅存 domain-model §2/§4.22 单处。验证：两守卫 EXIT=0、`check_requirements_authority.py` 6 项全绿。api-contract 体积约 37.7KB→23.8KB（约 -37%）。至此「解析/附件机制一个事实一个地方」三文档闭环：PRD 产品级（§6.5）+ api-contract 端点契约 + domain-model 唯一机制家（§2/§4.20~4.23）。剩余可选：E 守卫（锁 PRD 不回灌）、D 再平衡（7.3/7.4 产品意图 + domain-model §6 口径加厚）。
- docs(prd): PRD 详略再平衡第三批——§9 验收去复述，PRD 内解析/附件复制彻底收成单处。§9「建议验收覆盖」原把 REQ-AST-005/REQ-DOC-001/§6.5 整段二次声明（证号去重、附件 20MB/类型/下载日志墙、解析确认 16 条机制）当验收项。删复述留可测断言：证号去重压 1 行、附件保存墙压 1 行（5 门槛 + 行为见 §6.5/domain-model §4.22）、解析机制 16 条折成 1 条「全流程符合 §6.5 与 §2/§4.23」断言、删 1 条已折叠的失败重试项；保留 G1–G3 与创建重名拦截/面积拦截/模式校验/客户双指标抑制/`perm_admin` 授予边界/逾期通知数据范围等真·可测断言。至此解析/附件规则在 PRD 内由 5 处复制收成 §6.5 单处，grep 确认 PRD 全文已无 `20MB`/文件扩展名/`field_sources`/`ocr_prefill_*`/`manual_after_ocr_miss` 等机制 token——E 守卫（PRD 字段级 token 告警）落地前提已满足。PRD 体积约 50KB→33KB（约 -34%），章节骨架 §1~§11 完整、G1-G3 在位（ripgrep 复核）。验证：两守卫 EXIT=0、`check_requirements_authority.py` 6 项全绿、`check_field_drift` spec-only 13。下一批：api-contract §4.3/§4.10 跨文档复制收口（PRD 已净，机制墙现仅剩 api-contract 一处）、E 守卫、D 再平衡。

- feat(ledger): 落地 ADR-0011 / 2026-06-16 PRD Grill C3-C4。历史/分析聚合改读台账固化归属：产权方财务汇总读 `ContractLedgerEntry.attributed_owner_party_id`，台账资产筛选读 `attributed_asset_ids`，项目台账摘要和项目分析趋势按 `attributed_project_id` 取租金/服务费台账，全局经营分析 `project_breakdown` 按租金/服务费条目的固化项目拆分，当前资产/合同关系/租户/风险投影仍读当前归属。新增 `20260618_backfill_ledger_frozen_attribution.py` 回填迁移：租金台账按当前合同组项目/产权方/运营方回填，资产集合优先合同资产、空则回退合同组资产；服务费台账继承来源租金台账，且回填后仍缺项目/产权方/运营方/资产集合会失败暴露。补充 CRUD、ProjectService、AnalyticsService 和迁移测试。验证：`cd backend; .\.venv\Scripts\ruff.exe check ...` 与 `ruff format --check ...` 通过；定向 pytest 仍被当前 `backend/.venv` 指向缺失的 `C:\Users\ygz\AppData\Local\Programs\Python\Python313\python.exe` 阻塞。
- docs(domain-model,context): 收口 `all` 客户列表口径——`binding_type` 补 `all`、`historical_contract_count` 与分析客户双指标分离。grill 发现 §4.12 CustomerProfile `binding_type` 单值 `owner|manager` 与「客户列表按 `customer_party_id` 去重一客户一行」+ api-contract「常规客户列表可用 `all` 并集」矛盾：一个经 owner+manager 两绑定均可见的客户去重成一行后 binding_type 无值可填。yellowUp 同意：(1) `all` 列表一客户一行，`binding_type` 增 `all` 值（两绑定均可见的并集行）；(2) 明文区分——`historical_contract_count` 是列表/档案字段，按数据范围并集、合同 ID 去重计、`all` 合法、不受单视图约束；CONTEXT「客户双指标」单视图铁律只管分析端点会跨视图重复计数的 `customer_entity_count`/`customer_contract_count`，二者不得互相套用（同列表「内部」≠ 指标「内部」双义陷阱）。不新增 ADR（对既有客户双指标规则的收口、additive）。domain-model §4.12（binding_type + historical_contract_count 描述）、CONTEXT「客户双指标」词条+_Avoid_ 同步；后端 binding_type 增 all、档案计数走并集去重不套单视图守卫待实施。
- docs(adr,domain-model,context,prd): 主体历史身份稳定性——不合并主体 + 合同对手方名称定稿固化快照（ADR-0020），顺修 §4.18 Party 枚举 bug。grill Party 域浮出三处：(a) REQ-PTY-001「去重」语义未写死、无 merge 字段/端点；(b) §6.1「签署时主体名称快照」无字段承载（§4.7 只有 party_id 外键跟主档 live 变名，唯一沾边的 §4.8 tenant_name 只覆盖承租/下游，委托/直租漏，Party 改名会追溯改写历史合同显示名、违反「不追溯覆盖」）；(c) §4.18 Party `review_status` 抄串 Asset 列成 `reversed`，应为 `rejected`（CONTEXT「轻量提交标记」/§6 状态表均 `rejected`、Party 驳回后保留 `rejected` 态，可编辑后重新提审、不提供反审闭环）。yellowUp 同意。决策（新增 ADR-0020）：去重=建档查重提示、MVP 不合并主体（合并 live 去重会回溯改写历史客户口径撞 §3.2，归 vNext）；Contract 增 `lessor_name_snapshot`/`lessee_name_snapshot` 定稿固化、不随主档改名回写、覆盖全类型，`LeaseContractDetail.tenant_name` 退展示冗余从快照同步；当前身份 live / 历史引用冻结分层（同 ADR-0011）。domain-model §4.7 增两列快照、§4.18 修 `rejected`；PRD §6.1 引用快照字段+去重不合并、REQ-PTY-001 注「去重=建档查重」、§10 vNext 增「主体合并」；CONTEXT 新增「主体历史身份（不合并 + 名称快照固化）」词条；architecture README 索引；后端 Party 查重不引入合并、Contract 快照列+迁移+定稿写入+存量回填、tenant_name 改同步、api-contract 同步待实施。
- docs(domain-model,context,adr): 纠正 `已到期` 口径——派生展示状态、非操作，`expire` 不入审计（接上条 §4.16 收口）。grill 续核 §4.7 发现 `已到期` 明文「按 effective_to 派生」、只有 `已终止` 显式写入，那上一步保留进 `ContractAuditLog` 的 `expire` 动作自相矛盾（逾期从不产生「变逾期」审计事件，已到期同理非操作），且会诱导有人建到期 job 把 `生效` 物化成 `已到期` 落库、退回 ADR-0007 删掉的手工标记位老路。yellowUp 选 (A)：`已到期` 是派生覆盖层、单一真相轴是 `effective_to`。决策：`ContractAuditLog` action 再删 `expire`，最终集 = `{terminate, void, start_correction, finalize_correction}`；§4.7 `status` 标注存储轴只 `{草稿, 生效, 已终止}`、`已到期` 为派生展示状态非落库值（台账生成器直接比对 `effective_to` 停生成、无到期 job）。domain-model §4.7+§4.16、ADR-0013 影响段、CONTEXT「最小合同生命周期」词条+_Avoid_ 同步；后端审计枚举去 `expire`、确保无到期物化 job 待核。
- docs(domain-model,adr): 补完 ADR-0013 漏扫的 §4.16 ContractAuditLog 审批流残留。grill 发现 ADR-0013 删合同审批流时影响段只逐条列了 §4.7 Contract、漏列 §4.16 ContractAuditLog，致审计日志 action 枚举残留 `submit_review`/`approve`/`reject`/`reverse_review` 且仍带 `review_status_old`/`review_status_new` 字段——记录一套已被 ADR-0013 整删的 `ContractReviewStatus` 状态机转移，自相矛盾。yellowUp 同意补完清理（不新立 ADR，性质同 ADR-0013 正文「联审半边漏拆」）。决策：action 删四个审批流动作、保留 `expire`/`terminate`/`void`/`start_correction`，并补 `finalize_correction`（定稿触发台账重算/作废，§8 关键操作须审计，原 action 集缺定稿留痕）；删 `review_status_old/new` 字段，`old_status/new_status` 保留为生命周期状态转移。domain-model §4.16 表+约束段、ADR-0013 影响段（补 §4.16 条目）同步；后端审计枚举/字段收缩、补 `finalize_correction` 写入待实施。
- docs(context,domain-model): 显式化代理服务费实收口径——挂钩直租租金实收派生。grill 压 §4.15 `ServiceFeeLedger` 把 `amount_due`/`paid_amount`/`payment_status` 标「派生/同步来源租金台账」的承重假设（也是 ADR-0019 的前提）：模型 (A) 假定系统生成直租租金台账、有人登记其实收、服务费按比例派生；备选 (B) 服务费独立管理费旱涝保收、自带实收轴。yellowUp 确认 (A)——运营方与产权方对账，产权方实收到租金才结算代理费，服务费实收天然挂钩直租租金实收；运营方登记的「直租租金实收」反映对账确认的产权方到账结果（非运营方自己现金），解释了为何运营方在非自身收款主体的租金上登记实收。否决 (B)（与对账现实不符，且再开一条可漂移实收轴撞单一真相轴）。维持现有派生模型、不新增 ADR（确认既有模型 + 显式化口径，additive）。新增 CONTEXT「代理服务费实收（挂钩直租租金实收派生）」词条、domain-model §4.15 补约束段。
- docs(adr,context,domain-model): 台账 `unpaid/partial/paid` 改从 `paid_amount` 派生，实收登记只填金额（ADR-0019）。grill 核对消费方发现 `payment_status` 是残留双真相轴：逾期（ADR-0007）、收缴率/实收（domain-model §6）全按 `paid_amount` 算、不读 `payment_status`，唯一消费它的是 ADR-0008 重算跳过逻辑；而用户同时手填 `payment_status` 与 `paid_amount` 可漂移——登记 `paid` 但 `paid_amount=0` 时重算当已收跳过、逾期口径却标逾期、收缴率算没收，同一条目状态轴与金钱轴自相矛盾，是 `is_verified`(ADR-0005)/`overdue` 登记态(ADR-0007) 同型的可漂移手工标记位，侵蚀 §3.2 口径一致性。yellowUp 同意。决策（新增 ADR-0019）：`unpaid/partial/paid` 改为 `paid_amount` 对 `amount_due` 纯派生（==0→unpaid、0<…<amount_due→partial、>=amount_due→paid），实收登记只填 `paid_amount`+显式作废，`voided` 唯一非派生仅系统写；ADR-0008 跳过判据从读 `payment_status` 改读 `paid_amount > 0`；`ServiceFeeLedger` 同步。减免/结清少收属财务总账 Out of Scope，不靠翻状态、走 `amount_due` 更正。CONTEXT「逾期（派生口径）」「台账重算（已收条目不可变）」词条、domain-model §4.14、architecture README 索引同步；后端登记入参去 `payment_status`+状态改派生只读属性+跳过判据改 `paid_amount>0`+存量按 `paid_amount` 重派生迁移、前端去手选状态、api-contract、trace REQ-RNT-006 待实施。
- docs(adr,context): 收窄 ADR-0012「代理合同跨项目」为「委托协议跨项目」，确认客户双指标安全。grill 追问跨项目的合同类，yellowUp 确认：代理协议（委托，产权方↔运营方）会跨项目，租赁合同（直租/上游/下游）不会。据此 ADR-0012 泛称「代理合同」收窄为「委托协议」——直租恒单项目，故客户双指标（只数直租/下游 lessee 合同）的「按合同 ID 去重」逻辑正确、不会把跨项目兄弟记录算重、无需改按 `contract_number` 逻辑合同去重。CONTEXT「合同关系锁单项目」词条与 _Avoid_ 同步；ADR-0012 正文（背景+决策点2）、PRD §6.2/REQ-RNT-001 的泛称「代理合同」已统一改「委托协议」（直租/上游/下游恒单项目）。
